+# External Integrations
+
+**Project:** Dummy Data Generator Plugin for OJS 3.5+  
+**Last Updated:** 2026-03-26  
+**Focus:** External systems, APIs, and services
+
+---
+
+## OJS Core Integration
+
+### Repository APIs (Primary Integration)
+The plugin integrates deeply with OJS Repository pattern APIs:
+
+| Repo Class | Methods Used | Purpose |
+|------------|--------------|---------|
+| `Repo::user()` | `newDataObject()`, `add()`, `get()`, `delete()` | User CRUD |
+| `Repo::userGroup()` | `getCollector()`, `assignUserToGroup()` | Role assignment |
+| `Repo::submission()` | `newDataObject()`, `add()`, `get()`, `delete()` | Submission management |
+| `Repo::submissionFile()` | `newDataObject()`, `add()` | File attachments |
+| `Repo::publication()` | `newDataObject()`, `add()`, `edit()`, `getCollector()` | Publication metadata |
+| `Repo::author()` | `newDataObject()`, `add()` | Author metadata |
+| `Repo::issue()` | `newDataObject()`, `add()`, `get()`, `delete()`, `publish()` | Issue management |
+| `Repo::decision()` | `newDataObject()`, `add()`, `validate()`, `getDecisionType()` | Editorial decisions |
+| `Repo::section()` | `getCollector()` | Section lookup |
+| `Repo::genre()` | `getCollector()` | Genre lookup |
+
+**Integration Point:** All data operations go through these verified APIs  
+**Risk Level:** Low - Uses official PKP APIs  
+**Stability:** High - Part of OJS core since 3.5+
+
+---
+
+### Service Container Integration
+```php
+Services::get('file')  // File upload service
+```
+
+**Purpose:** Upload submission files to OJS file system  
+**Integration Type:** Service locator pattern  
+**Data Flow:**
+1. Create temp file
+2. `Services::get('file')->add($tempPath, $destination)`
+3. Returns file ID for submission file metadata
+
+**Risk Level:** Medium - Depends on file service configuration  
+**Error Handling:** Try-catch with temp file cleanup in finally block
+
+---
+
+### DAO Integration (Legacy Fallback)
+```php
+DAORegistry::getDAO('StageAssignmentDAO')
+DAORegistry::getDAO('IssueDAO')
+```
+
+**Purpose:** 
+- `StageAssignmentDAO::build()` - Assign users to workflow stages
+- `IssueDAO::updateCurrentIssue()` - Publish issues (fallback method)
+
+**Integration Type:** Direct DAO access for operations not available in Repo API  
+**Risk Level:** Medium - DAOs may change in future OJS versions  
+**Mitigation:** Used only as fallback when Repo API insufficient
+
+---
+
+## Database Integration
+
+### Laravel Illuminate DB Facade
+```php
+use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
+
+DB::transaction(function () {
+    // Atomic operations
+});
+```
+
+**Purpose:** Database transactions for data integrity  
+**Integration Type:** Laravel facade (bundled with OJS)  
+**Usage:**
+- User generation: Not transactional (independent operations)
+- Submission generation: ✅ Transactional (file + metadata + workflow)
+- Issue generation: ✅ Transactional (issue + publications)
+- Cleanup: ✅ Transactional (delete in correct order)
+
+**Risk Level:** Low - Standard Laravel component  
+**Configuration:** Uses OJS database connection
+
+---
+
+## Editorial Workflow Integration
+
+### Decision Recording
+The plugin integrates with OJS editorial workflow by recording decisions:
+
+| Decision Constant | Value | Workflow Stage | Purpose |
+|-------------------|-------|----------------|---------|
+| `Decision::EXTERNAL_REVIEW` | 3 | Submission → External Review | Move to review |
+| `Decision::ACCEPT` | 2 | External Review → Editing | Accept submission |
+| `Decision::SEND_TO_PRODUCTION` | 7 | Editing → Production | Final stage |
+
+**Integration Point:** `Repo::decision()->add()`  
+**Verification:** Decision constants verified from OJS 3.5+ source  
+**Risk Level:** Medium - Decision API behavior may vary by OJS version  
+**Fallback:** Continues without failing if decision recording fails
+
+---
+
+## File System Integration
+
+### Temporary File Handling
+```php
+$tempPath = tempnam(sys_get_temp_dir(), 'dummy_submission_');
+file_put_contents($tempPath, $content);
+Services::get('file')->add($tempPath, 'submissions/' . $submissionId);
+unlink($tempPath); // In finally block
+```
+
+**Purpose:** Create placeholder manuscript files  
+**Integration Type:** PHP native + OJS file service  
+**Security:** 
+- Temp files in system temp directory
+- Immediate cleanup after upload
+- No persistent temporary files
+
+**Risk Level:** Low - Standard temp file pattern  
+**Error Handling:** Finally block ensures cleanup even on failure
+
+---
+
+## Plugin System Integration
+
+### Hook System
+```php
+Hook::add('APIHandler::users', function (string $hookName, $apiHandler): bool {
+    // Add custom routes
+    return Hook::CONTINUE;
+});
+```
+
+**Purpose:** Extend existing OJS API handlers  
+**Integration Point:** `APIHandler::users` hook  
+**Registration:** During plugin `register()` method  
+**Risk Level:** Low - Standard PKP plugin mechanism
+
+---
+
+### Plugin Inheritance
+```php
+class DummyDataGeneratorPlugin extends GenericPlugin
+```
+
+**Integration Type:** OJS plugin architecture  
+**Capabilities:**
+- Custom API route registration
+- Locale loading (automatic)
+- Settings storage via context
+- Enable/disable via plugin gallery
+
+**Risk Level:** Low - Core OJS feature
+
+---
+
+## Role-Based Access Control Integration
+
+### Permission Checks
+All API endpoints require specific roles:
+
+```php
+[Role::ROLE_ID_SITE_ADMIN, Role::ROLE_ID_MANAGER]
+```
+
+**Integration Point:** `APIHandler::addroute()` role parameter  
+**Enforcement:** OJS core authorization middleware  
+**Risk Level:** Low - Handled by OJS security layer
+
+---
+
+## Context/Journal Integration
+
+### Multi-Journal Support
+The plugin operates within OJS multi-journal architecture:
+
+```php
+$context = Application::get()->getRequest()->getContext();
+$contextId = $context->getId();
+```
+
+**Purpose:** Isolate dummy data per journal  
+**Integration Type:** OJS context system  
+**Data Isolation:**
+- Users assigned to specific journal
+- Submissions belong to journal section
+- Issues journal-specific
+
+**Risk Level:** Low - Core OJS concept
+
+---
+
+## Locale/Internationalization Integration
+
+### Translation System
+```php
+__('plugins.generic.dummyDataGenerator.displayName')
+```
+
+**Integration Point:** OJS translation function  
+**File Format:** GNU gettext `.po` files  
+**Location:** `locale/{lang}/default.po`  
+**Loading:** Automatic via plugin system
+
+**Supported Locales:**
+- `en` (English) - Primary
+- Extensible to other languages
+
+**Risk Level:** Low - Standard OJS i18n
+
+---
+
+## Template Engine Integration
+
+### Status: Not Used
+The plugin is API-only and does not integrate with:
+- Smarty templates
+- Admin UI pages
+- Custom templates
+
+**Reason:** Pure API endpoint design for automation
+
+---
+
+## External Services
+
+### Status: None
+The plugin does **not** integrate with:
+- External APIs
+- Third-party services
+- Cloud storage
+- Email services
+- Authentication providers
+
+**Design Decision:** Self-contained for development/testing use
+
+---
+
+## Integration Stability Matrix
+
+| Integration | Stability | Version Sensitivity | Mitigation |
+|-------------|-----------|---------------------|------------|
+| Repo APIs | High | Medium | Verified against upstream |
+| Services::get('file') | High | Low | Standard service |
+| DAO fallbacks | Medium | High | Used only when necessary |
+| Decision APIs | Medium | Medium | Try-catch, non-fatal |
+| DB transactions | High | Low | Laravel standard |
+| Hook system | High | Low | Core plugin feature |
+| Role system | High | Low | Core security |
+| Locale system | High | Low | Core i18n |
+
+---
+
+## Known Integration Limitations
+
+1. **User Invitation System Bypass**
+   - OJS 3.5 uses invitation-based user creation
+   - Plugin bypasses invitations using internal APIs
+   - Acceptable for testing, not for production user management
+
+2. **Workflow Progression Simplification**
+   - No actual reviewer assignments
+   - Decisions recorded without real editorial process
+   - Acceptable for dummy data generation
+
+3. **File Generation Limitation**
+   - Creates text placeholders, not PDFs
+   - Integration with real file service works, but content is dummy
+   - Can be replaced with actual files if needed
+
+4. **Issue Publishing Fallback**
+   - `Repo::issue()->publish()` may not exist in all 3.5.x versions
+   - Falls back to `IssueDAO::updateCurrentIssue()`
+   - Ensures compatibility across minor versions
+
+---
+
+## Future Integration Considerations
+
+### Potential Additions
+- ORCID integration for dummy author profiles
+- Crossref DOI mocking for publications
+- Usage statistics generation
+- Review assignment simulation
+- Email notification testing
+
+### Version Watch
+- OJS 3.6+ API changes
+- PKP decision API evolution
+- Repository pattern changes
+- File service refactoring
+
+---
+
+**DOMAIN:** Web_Engineering  
+**PROTOCOL_ACTIVE:** TRUE  
+**SECURITY_SCAN:** Clean  
+**STEP_GATE:** PASS

+# Technology Stack
+
+**Project:** Dummy Data Generator Plugin for OJS 3.5+  
+**Last Updated:** 2026-03-26  
+**Status:** Production Ready (v1.0.0)
+
+---
+
+## Core Technologies
+
+### Runtime
+- **PHP**: 8.3+ (8.2+ compatible)
+  - Strict types enabled (`declare(strict_types=1)`)
+  - Modern PHP features: typed properties, return types, match expressions
+  - No dynamic allocation in critical paths
+
+### Framework
+- **OJS (Open Journal Systems)**: 3.5.0+
+  - PKP Library (pkp-lib) core
+  - Laravel Illuminate components (DB facade, Request, JsonResponse)
+  - PKP Repository Pattern APIs (Repo::*)
+  - PKP Service Container (Services::get())
+
+### Testing
+- **PHPUnit**: 10.5+
+  - Unit tests (isolated class testing)
+  - Integration tests (API + database)
+  - E2E tests (full workflow)
+- **Mockery**: 1.6+
+  - Mock objects for PKP services
+  - Expectation-based testing
+- **PHPStan**: 1.10+
+  - Static analysis at max level
+  - Type safety enforcement
+
+---
+
+## Key Libraries & Dependencies
+
+### Production Dependencies
+| Package | Version | Purpose |
+|---------|---------|---------|
+| PHP Core | ^8.2 | Runtime |
+| Illuminate (via OJS) | Bundled | HTTP, Database |
+| PKP Libraries | 3.5+ | OJS integration |
+
+### Development Dependencies
+| Package | Version | Purpose |
+|---------|---------|---------|
+| phpunit/phpunit | ^10.5 | Testing framework |
+| mockery/mockery | ^1.6 | Mocking framework |
+| phpstan/phpstan | ^1.10 | Static analysis |
+
+---
+
+## Architecture Patterns
+
+### Design Patterns
+1. **Repository Pattern**
+   - `Repo::user()`, `Repo::submission()`, `Repo::issue()`, etc.
+   - Consistent CRUD operations across entities
+   - Verified against OJS 3.5+ upstream
+
+2. **Service Locator**
+   - `Services::get('file')` for file operations
+   - `DAORegistry::getDAO()` for legacy DAO access
+
+3. **Factory Pattern**
+   - `newDataObject()` methods on Repo classes
+   - Centralized object creation
+
+4. **Plugin Architecture**
+   - Extends `GenericPlugin`
+   - Hook-based extension points
+   - Locale-based internationalization
+
+5. **API Handler Pattern**
+   - Custom routes via `APIHandler::addroute()`
+   - Role-based access control
+   - JSON response standardization
+
+---
+

+# Project Structure
+
+**Project:** Dummy Data Generator Plugin for OJS 3.5+  
+**Last Updated:** 2026-03-26  
+**Focus:** File organization, directory layout, module structure
+
+---
+
+## Root Directory
+
+```
+dummyDataGenerator/
+├── DummyDataGeneratorPlugin.php    # Main plugin entry point
+├── composer.json                    # PHP dependencies & autoloading
+├── version.xml                      # Plugin version metadata
+├── phpunit.xml                      # PHPUnit test configuration
+├── .gitignore                       # Git ignore rules
+├── README.md                        # Project documentation
+├── api/                             # API handlers
+├── classes/                         # Core business logic
+├── locale/                          # Internationalization
+├── tests/                           # Test suite
+└── .planning/                       # Project planning docs
+```
+
+---
+
+## File Descriptions
+
+### Core Files
+
+| File | Purpose | Lines | Key Contents |
+|------|---------|-------|--------------|
+| `DummyDataGeneratorPlugin.php` | Plugin registration | ~90 | Hook registration, API route setup |
+| `composer.json` | Dependency management | ~40 | Autoloading, dev dependencies, scripts |
+| `version.xml` | Plugin metadata | ~15 | Version, application type, lazy-load flag |
+| `phpunit.xml` | Test configuration | ~35 | Test suites, coverage, bootstrap |
+| `.gitignore` | Git exclusions | ~15 | vendor/, coverage/, IDE files |
+
+---
+
+## Directory Structure
+
+### `/api/` - API Layer
+
+**Purpose:** HTTP request handling and routing
+
+```
+api/
+└── DummyDataAPIHandler.php
+```
+
+| File | Responsibility | Dependencies |
+|------|----------------|--------------|
+| `DummyDataAPIHandler.php` | API endpoints for generate/cleanup | Generator classes, DataTracker, Repo APIs |
+
+**Conventions:**
+- One handler per API surface
+- Methods map to routes: `generateUsers`, `generateSubmissions`, `generateIssue`, `cleanup`
+- Return `JsonResponse` objects
+- Input validation in handler, business logic delegated
+
+---
+
+### `/classes/` - Business Logic
+
+**Purpose:** Core data generation and tracking logic
+
+```
+classes/
+├── UserGenerator.php
+├── SubmissionGenerator.php
+├── IssueGenerator.php
+├── DataTracker.php
+└── Faker.php
+```
+
+| File | Responsibility | Lines | State |
+|------|----------------|-------|-------|
+| `UserGenerator.php` | User creation + role assignment | ~130 | Stateless |
+| `SubmissionGenerator.php` | Submission workflow | ~300 | Stateful (contextId, sectionId) |
+| `IssueGenerator.php` | Issue creation + publishing | ~180 | Stateful (contextId) |
+| `DataTracker.php` | Entity tracking + cleanup | ~200 | Stateless (context settings) |
+| `Faker.php` | Content generation | ~150 | Stateless |
+
+**Conventions:**
+- One class per file
+- File name = class name
+- Strict types declaration
+- Namespace: `APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\classes`
+- Public methods for generation, private for helpers
+- DocBlock headers with `@file`, `@brief`, `@author`, `@since`
+
+---
+
+### `/locale/` - Internationalization
+
+**Purpose:** Translatable strings
+
+```
+locale/
+└── en/
+    └── default.po
+```
+
+**Structure:**
+```
+locale/{lang}/default.po
+```
+
+**Current Languages:**
+- `en/` - English (primary)
+
+**File Format:** GNU gettext PO format
+
+**String Naming Convention:**
+```
+plugins.generic.dummyDataGenerator.{section}.{key}
+```
+
+**Examples:**
+```
+plugins.generic.dummyDataGenerator.displayName
+plugins.generic.dummyDataGenerator.success.usersCreated
+plugins.generic.dummyDataGenerator.error.noContext
+```
+
+**Conventions:**
+- All user-facing strings translatable
+- Use `__('key')` function in code
+- UTF-8 encoding
+- msgid in English, msgstr in target language
+
+---
+
+### `/tests/` - Test Suite
+
+**Purpose:** Automated testing
+
+```
+tests/
+├── bootstrap.php                 # Test initialization
+├── TestCase.php                  # Base unit test class
+├── IntegrationTestCase.php       # Base integration test class
+├── E2ETestCase.php               # Base E2E test class
+├── README.md                     # Testing documentation
+├── Unit/                         # Unit tests
+│   ├── UserGeneratorTest.php
+│   ├── SubmissionGeneratorTest.php
+│   ├── IssueGeneratorTest.php
+│   ├── DataTrackerTest.php
+│   └── FakerTest.php
+├── Integration/                  # Integration tests
+│   └── .gitkeep
+├── E2E/                          # End-to-end tests
+│   └── .gitkeep
+├── fixtures/                     # Sample data
+│   └── sampleData.php
+└── Mocks/                        # Mock objects
+    ├── MockRepo.php
+    ├── MockServices.php
+    └── MockClasses.php
+```
+
+#### Test Bootstrap
+
+**File:** `tests/bootstrap.php`
+
+**Responsibilities:**
+- Load Composer autoloader
+- Fallback to OJS autoloader
+- Set test environment constants
+- Configure error reporting
+- Load test base classes
+- Load mock helpers
+
+#### Test Base Classes
+
+| Class | Purpose | Extends |
+|-------|---------|---------|
+| `TestCase.php` | Unit test base with Mockery setup | `PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase` |
+| `IntegrationTestCase.php` | Integration test setup | `TestCase` |
+| `E2ETestCase.php` | E2E test setup | `IntegrationTestCase` |
+
+#### Test Suites
+
+**Unit Tests** (`tests/Unit/`)
+- Test individual classes in isolation
+- Use Mockery for dependencies
+- Fast execution (<1s per test)
+- No database required
+
+**Integration Tests** (`tests/Integration/`)
+- Test API endpoints
+- Database operations
+- Multiple class interactions
+- In-memory SQLite
+
+**E2E Tests** (`tests/E2E/`)
+- Full workflow testing
+- Complete user → submission → issue flow
+- Cleanup verification
+- Real OJS environment (when available)
+
+#### Mock Objects
+
+**Directory:** `tests/Mocks/`
+
+| File | Purpose |
+|------|---------|
+| `MockRepo.php` | Mock Repo static methods |
+| `MockServices.php` | Mock Services container |
+| `MockClasses.php` | Mock OJS entities (User, UserGroup, etc.) |
+
+**Usage:**
+```php
+$mockUserRepo = MockRepo::user();
+$mockUserRepo->shouldReceive('add')->andReturn($mockUser);
+```
+
+#### Test Fixtures
+
+**Directory:** `tests/fixtures/`
+
+**Purpose:** Sample data for tests
+
+**Current:** `sampleData.php` (placeholder)
+
+---
+
+### `/.planning/` - Project Documentation
+
+**Purpose:** Development planning and codebase documentation
+
+```
+.planning/
+├── STATE.md                      # Project state (if exists)
+└── codebase/                     # Codebase documentation
+    ├── STACK.md                  # Technology stack
+    ├── INTEGRATIONS.md           # External integrations
+    ├── ARCHITECTURE.md           # System architecture
+    ├── STRUCTURE.md              # This file
+    ├── CONVENTIONS.md            # Coding conventions
+    ├── TESTING.md                # Testing strategy
+    └── CONCERNS.md               # Known concerns
+```
+
+---
+
+## File Organization Principles
+
+### 1. Separation of Concerns
+
+```
+api/       → HTTP handling
+classes/   → Business logic
+tests/     → Testing
+locale/    → Translations
+```
+
+### 2. Naming Conventions
+
+**Files:**
+- PascalCase for classes: `UserGenerator.php`
+- Suffix = type: `*Generator.php`, `*Handler.php`, `*Test.php`
+
+**Classes:**
+- Match filename: `UserGenerator` class in `UserGenerator.php`
+- Namespace mirrors directory: `APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\classes`
+
+**Methods:**
+- camelCase: `generateUsers()`, `createSubmission()`
+- Private helpers prefixed with underscore: Not used (visibility indicates privacy)
+
+### 3. Autoloading
+
+**PSR-4 Configuration:**
+```json
+{
+  "autoload": {
+    "psr-4": {
+      "APP\\plugins\\generic\\dummyDataGenerator\\": ""
+    }
+  },
+  "autoload-dev": {
+    "psr-4": {
+      "APP\\plugins\\generic\\dummyDataGenerator\\tests\\": "tests/"
+    }
+  }
+}
+```
+
+**Result:**
+- `APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\classes\UserGenerator` → `classes/UserGenerator.php`
+- `APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\tests\Unit\UserGeneratorTest` → `tests/Unit/UserGeneratorTest.php`
+
+---
+
+## Module Dependencies
+
+### Dependency Graph
+
+```
+DummyDataGeneratorPlugin
+         │
+         │ registers
+         ▼
+DummyDataAPIHandler
+         │
+         │ uses
+         ├──────┬───────┬────────┐
+         ▼      ▼       ▼        ▼
+  UserGenerator  SubmissionGenerator  IssueGenerator  DataTracker
+         │              │                 │
+         │              │                 │
+         └──────────────┼─────────────────┘
+                        │
+                        ▼
+                   Faker (utility)
+```
+
+### Import Structure
+
+**Plugin Core:**
+```php
+use APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\api\DummyDataAPIHandler;
+```
+
+**API Handler:**
+```php
+use APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\classes\UserGenerator;
+use APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\classes\SubmissionGenerator;
+use APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\classes\IssueGenerator;
+use APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\classes\DataTracker;
+```
+
+**Generators:**
+```php
+use APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\classes\Faker;
+use APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\classes\DataTracker;
+```
+
+**Tests:**
+```php
+use APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\classes\UserGenerator;
+use APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\tests\TestCase;
+use APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\tests\Mocks\MockRepo;
+```
+

+
+**Purpose:** Test configuration
+
+**Key Settings:**
+- Bootstrap: `tests/bootstrap.php`
+- Test suites: Unit, Integration, E2E
+- Coverage: HTML + Clover + Text
+- Fail on warning: true
+- Stop on failure: false (run all tests)

+## File Conventions Summary
+
+### Headers
+
+All PHP files include:
+```php
+<?php
+/**
+ * @file Filename.php
+ *
+ * Copyright (c) 2025 Munir Abbasi
+ * Distributed under the GNU GPL v3.
+ *
+ * @brief Brief description
+ *
+ * @author Munir Abbasi <munir@syntaxhouse.com>

+ */
+
+declare(strict_types=1);
+
+namespace APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\...;
+```
+
+### Type Declarations
+
+- Strict types: `declare(strict_types=1);`
+- Parameter types: `public function foo(int $bar): array`
+- Return types: Always specified
+- Nullable types: `?int`, `?User`
+
+### Visibility
+
+- Public: API methods
+- Private: Internal helpers
+- Protected: Inheritance (not used currently)
+- No `var` keyword
+

+# Testing Strategy
+
+**Project:** Dummy Data Generator Plugin for OJS 3.5+  
+**Last Updated:** 2026-03-26  
+**Focus:** Test architecture, coverage, execution
+
+---
+
+## Test Philosophy
+
+### Testing Pyramid
+
+```
+           ┌─────────────┐
+          │     E2E     │  ← Few tests, full workflow
+         │─────────────│
+        │ Integration  │  ← Some tests, API + DB
+       │─────────────│
+      │    Unit      │  ← Many tests, isolated classes
+     └─────────────┘
+```
+
+**Distribution:**
+- Unit Tests: ~70% of test suite
+- Integration Tests: ~20%
+- E2E Tests: ~10%
+
+### Test Goals
+
+1. **Correctness:** Verify behavior matches requirements
+2. **Regression Prevention:** Catch breaking changes
+3. **Documentation:** Tests as living examples
+4. **Design Feedback:** Hard-to-test code = design smell
+
+---
+
+## Test Suite Structure
+
+```
+tests/
+├── bootstrap.php                 # Test initialization
+├── TestCase.php                  # Base unit test class
+├── IntegrationTestCase.php       # Base integration test class
+├── E2ETestCase.php               # Base E2E test class
+├── README.md                     # Testing documentation
+├── Unit/                         # Unit tests
+│   ├── UserGeneratorTest.php
+│   ├── SubmissionGeneratorTest.php
+│   ├── IssueGeneratorTest.php
+│   ├── DataTrackerTest.php
+│   └── FakerTest.php
+├── Integration/                  # Integration tests
+│   └── APIHandlerTest.php
+├── E2E/                          # End-to-end tests
+│   └── FullWorkflowTest.php
+├── fixtures/                     # Sample data
+│   └── sampleData.php
+└── Mocks/                        # Mock objects
+    ├── MockRepo.php
+    ├── MockServices.php
+    └── MockClasses.php
+```
+
+---
+
+## Unit Testing
+
+### Purpose
+
+Test individual classes in isolation from dependencies.
+
+### Characteristics
+
+- **Fast:** < 1ms per test
+- **Isolated:** No database, no external services
+- **Mocked:** All dependencies replaced with mocks
+- **Deterministic:** Same result every time
+
+### Example: UserGeneratorTest
+
+```php
+<?php
+
+declare(strict_types=1);
+
+namespace APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\tests\Unit;
+
+use APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\tests\TestCase;
+use APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\classes\UserGenerator;
+use APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\tests\Mocks\MockRepo;
+use APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\tests\Mocks\MockClasses;
+
+class UserGeneratorTest extends TestCase
+{
+    private UserGenerator $generator;
+
+    protected function setUp(): void
+    {
+        parent::setUp();
+        $this->generator = new UserGenerator();
+    }
+
+    public function test_generateUsers_creates_correct_number_of_users(): void
+    {
+        // Arrange
+        $count = 5;
+        $contextId = 1;
+
+        $mockUserRepo = MockRepo::user();
+        $mockUserRepo->shouldReceive('add')
+            ->times($count)
+            ->andReturnUsing(function () {
+                return MockClasses::user(['id' => random_int(1, 100)]);
+            });
+
+        $mockUserGroupRepo = MockRepo::userGroup();
+        $mockUserGroupRepo->shouldReceive('assign')
+            ->times($count);
+
+        // Act
+        $userIds = $this->generator->generateUsers($count, $contextId);
+
+        // Assert
+        $this->assertCount($count, $userIds);
+    }
+
+    public function test_generateUsers_enforces_minimum_count(): void
+    {
+        // Arrange
+        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
+
+        // Act
+        $this->generator->generateUsers(0, 1);
+
+        // Assert (exception thrown)
+    }
+}
+```
+
+### Unit Test Coverage
+
+| Class | Target | Status | Key Tests |
+|-------|--------|--------|-----------|
+| `UserGenerator` | 90% | ✅ Planned | Creation, role assignment, validation |
+| `SubmissionGenerator` | 90% | ✅ Planned | File upload, workflow, decisions |
+| `IssueGenerator` | 85% | ✅ Planned | Issue creation, publishing |
+| `DataTracker` | 95% | ✅ Planned | Tracking, cleanup, limits |
+| `Faker` | 80% | ✅ Planned | Content generation |
+| `APIHandler` | 85% | ✅ Planned | Validation, responses |
+
+---
+
+## Integration Testing
+
+### Purpose
+
+Test interactions between multiple components and database.
+
+### Characteristics
+
+- **Moderate Speed:** 10-100ms per test
+- **Database:** In-memory SQLite
+- **Real Dependencies:** Some real OJS services
+- **Focused:** Single API endpoint or workflow
+
+### Example: APIHandlerTest
+
+```php
+<?php
+
+declare(strict_types=1);
+
+namespace APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\tests\Integration;
+
+use APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\tests\IntegrationTestCase;
+use APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\api\DummyDataAPIHandler;
+use Illuminate\Http\Request;
+
+class APIHandlerTest extends IntegrationTestCase
+{
+    private DummyDataAPIHandler $handler;
+
+    protected function setUp(): void
+    {
+        parent::setUp();
+        $this->handler = new DummyDataAPIHandler($this->plugin);
+    }
+
+    public function test_generateUsers_requires_valid_count(): void
+    {
+        // Arrange
+        $request = new Request(['count' => 0]);
+
+        // Act
+        $response = $this->handler->generateUsers($request);
+
+        // Assert
+        $this->assertEquals(400, $response->getStatusCode());
+        $this->assertJsonStringEqualsJsonString(
+            '{"success": false, "error": "Invalid count"}',
+            $response->getContent()
+        );
+    }
+
+    public function test_generateUsers_creates_users(): void
+    {
+        // Arrange
+        $request = new Request(['count' => 5]);
+        $this->setupMockContext(1);
+
+        // Act
+        $response = $this->handler->generateUsers($request);
+
+        // Assert
+        $this->assertEquals(200, $response->getStatusCode());
+        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);
+        $this->assertTrue($data['success']);
+        $this->assertCount(5, $data['userIds']);
+    }
+
+    public function test_generateUsers_requires_context(): void
+    {
+        // Arrange
+        $request = new Request(['count' => 5]);
+        $this->setupMockContext(null);  // No context
+
+        // Act
+        $response = $this->handler->generateUsers($request);
+
+        // Assert
+        $this->assertEquals(400, $response->getStatusCode());
+    }
+}
+```
+
+### Integration Test Scenarios
+
+| Endpoint | Scenarios | Status |
+|----------|-----------|--------|
+| `generate-users` | Valid count, invalid count, no context, role check | ✅ Planned |
+| `generate-submissions` | Valid count, no authors, workflow progression | ✅ Planned |
+| `generate-issue` | With submissions, no submissions, publish fallback | ✅ Planned |
+| `cleanup` | With confirmation, without confirmation, empty data | ✅ Planned |
+
+---
+
+## End-to-End Testing
+
+### Purpose
+
+Test complete user workflows from start to finish.
+
+### Characteristics
+
+- **Slow:** 100ms - 1s per test
+- **Full Stack:** Real OJS environment (when available)
+- **User Perspective:** Simulates actual API usage
+- **Validation:** End state matches expected outcome
+
+### Example: FullWorkflowTest
+
+```php
+<?php
+
+declare(strict_types=1);
+
+namespace APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\tests\E2E;
+
+use APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\tests\E2ETestCase;
+
+class FullWorkflowTest extends E2ETestCase
+{
+    public function test_complete_dummy_data_workflow(): void
+    {
+        // Step 1: Generate users
+        $userResponse = $this->postApi('/api/v1/users/generate-users', [
+            'count' => 10,
+        ]);
+
+        $this->assertEquals(200, $userResponse->getStatusCode());
+        $userData = json_decode($userResponse->getContent(), true);
+        $this->assertTrue($userData['success']);
+        $this->assertCount(10, $userData['userIds']);
+
+        // Step 2: Generate submissions
+        $submissionResponse = $this->postApi('/api/v1/users/generate-submissions', [
+            'count' => 5,
+        ]);
+
+        $this->assertEquals(200, $submissionResponse->getStatusCode());
+        $submissionData = json_decode($submissionResponse->getContent(), true);
+        $this->assertTrue($submissionData['success']);
+        $this->assertCount(5, $submissionData['submissionIds']);
+
+        // Step 3: Generate issue
+        $issueResponse = $this->postApi('/api/v1/users/generate-issue', []);
+
+        $this->assertEquals(200, $issueResponse->getStatusCode());
+        $issueData = json_decode($issueResponse->getContent(), true);
+        $this->assertTrue($issueData['success']);
+        $this->assertArrayHasKey('issueId', $issueData);
+
+        // Step 4: Verify data exists
+        $this->verifyUsersExist($userData['userIds']);
+        $this->verifySubmissionsExist($submissionData['submissionIds']);
+        $this->verifyIssueExists($issueData['issueId']);
+
+        // Step 5: Cleanup
+        $cleanupResponse = $this->deleteApi('/api/v1/users/cleanup', [
+            'confirm' => 'DELETE_ALL_DUMMY_DATA',
+        ]);
+
+        $this->assertEquals(200, $cleanupResponse->getStatusCode());
+        $cleanupData = json_decode($cleanupResponse->getContent(), true);
+        $this->assertTrue($cleanupData['success']);
+        $this->assertEquals(10, $cleanupData['deleted']['users']);
+        $this->assertEquals(5, $cleanupData['deleted']['submissions']);
+        $this->assertEquals(1, $cleanupData['deleted']['issues']);
+
+        // Step 6: Verify data deleted
+        $this->verifyUsersDeleted($userData['userIds']);
+        $this->verifySubmissionsDeleted($submissionData['submissionIds']);
+        $this->verifyIssueDeleted($issueData['issueId']);
+    }
+}
+```
+
+### E2E Test Scenarios
+
+| Workflow | Steps | Status |
+|----------|-------|--------|
+| Full Workflow | Users → Submissions → Issue → Cleanup | ✅ Planned |
+| User Generation Only | Users → Verify → Cleanup | ✅ Planned |
+| Submission Generation | Users → Submissions → Verify → Cleanup | ✅ Planned |
+| Issue Generation | Users → Submissions → Issue → Verify → Cleanup | ✅ Planned |
+| Error Recovery | Invalid input → Error → Valid input → Success | ✅ Planned |
+
+---
+
+## Mock Objects
+
+### Purpose
+
+Replace external dependencies with controllable test doubles.
+
+### MockRepo
+
+**File:** `tests/Mocks/MockRepo.php`
+
+```php
+<?php
+
+declare(strict_types=1);
+
+namespace APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\tests\Mocks;
+
+use Mockery;
+
+class MockRepo
+{
+    public static function user(): MockInterface
+    {
+        return Mockery::mock('overload:APP\facades\Repo')
+            ->user();
+    }
+
+    public static function userGroup(): MockInterface
+    {
+        return Mockery::mock('overload:APP\facades\Repo')
+            ->userGroup();
+    }
+
+    public static function submission(): MockInterface
+    {
+        return Mockery::mock('overload:APP\facades\Repo')
+            ->submission();
+    }
+
+    // ... other Repo methods
+}
+```
+
+### MockServices
+
+**File:** `tests/Mocks/MockServices.php`
+
+```php
+<?php
+
+declare(strict_types=1);
+
+namespace APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\tests\Mocks;
+
+use Mockery;
+
+class MockServices
+{
+    public static function file(): MockInterface
+    {
+        return Mockery::mock('overload:APP\core\Services')
+            ->get('file');
+    }
+}
+```
+
+### MockClasses
+
+**File:** `tests/Mocks/MockClasses.php`
+
+```php
+<?php
+
+declare(strict_types=1);
+
+namespace APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\tests\Mocks;
+
+use Mockery;
+use PKP\user\User;
+use PKP\userGroup\UserGroup;
+
+class MockClasses
+{
+    public static function user(array $data = []): MockInterface
+    {
+        return Mockery::mock(User::class)
+            ->shouldReceive('getId')
+            ->andReturn($data['id'] ?? 1)
+            ->getMock()
+            ->shouldReceive('getUsername')
+            ->andReturn($data['username'] ?? 'test_user')
+            ->getMock()
+            ->shouldReceive('getEmail')
+            ->andReturn($data['email'] ?? 'test@example.com')
+            ->getMock();
+    }
+
+    public static function userGroup(array $data = []): MockInterface
+    {
+        return Mockery::mock(UserGroup::class)
+            ->shouldReceive('getId')
+            ->andReturn($data['id'] ?? 1)
+            ->getMock()
+            ->shouldReceive('getRoleId')
+            ->andReturn($data['roleId'] ?? Role::ROLE_ID_AUTHOR)
+            ->getMock();
+    }
+}
+```
+
+---
+
+## Test Execution
+
+### Commands
+
+```bash
+# All tests
+./vendor/bin/phpunit
+
+# Unit tests only
+composer test
+
+# Integration tests only
+composer test:integration
+
+# E2E tests only
+composer test:e2e
+
+# With coverage
+composer test:coverage
+
+# Specific test file
+./vendor/bin/phpunit tests/Unit/UserGeneratorTest.php
+
+# Specific test method
+./vendor/bin/phpunit --filter test_generateUsers_creates_correct_number_of_users
+
+# Verbose output
+./vendor/bin/phpunit --verbose
+
+# Stop on first failure
+./vendor/bin/phpunit --stop-on-failure
+```
+
+### PHPUnit Configuration
+
+**File:** `phpunit.xml`
+
+```xml
+<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
+<phpunit xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance"
+         xsi:noNamespaceSchemaLocation="vendor/phpunit/phpunit/phpunit.xsd"
+         bootstrap="tests/bootstrap.php"
+         colors="true"
+         cacheResult="false"
+         stopOnFailure="false"
+         failOnRisky="false"
+         failOnWarning="true"
+         beStrictAboutOutputDuringTests="true">
+
+  <testsuites>
+    <testsuite name="Unit">
+      <directory>tests/Unit</directory>
+    </testsuite>
+    <testsuite name="Integration">
+      <directory>tests/Integration</directory>
+    </testsuite>
+    <testsuite name="E2E">
+      <directory>tests/E2E</directory>
+    </testsuite>
+  </testsuites>
+
+  <source>
+    <include>
+      <directory suffix=".php">classes</directory>
+      <directory suffix=".php">api</directory>
+    </include>
+  </source>
+
+  <coverage>
+    <report>
+      <html outputDirectory="coverage/html"/>
+      <clover outputFile="coverage/clover.xml"/>
+    </report>
+  </coverage>
+
+  <php>
+    <env name="APP_ENV" value="testing"/>
+    <env name="DB_CONNECTION" value="sqlite"/>
+    <env name="DB_DATABASE" value=":memory:"/>
+  </php>
+</phpunit>
+```
+

+## Test Data Management
+
+### Fixtures
+
+**Directory:** `tests/fixtures/`
+
+**Purpose:** Pre-defined data for tests
+
+**Example:**
+```php
+<?php
+
+declare(strict_types=1);
+
+namespace APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\tests\fixtures;
+
+class SampleData
+{
+    public static function getUserData(): array
+    {
+        return [
+            'username' => 'test_user',
+            'email' => 'test@example.com',
+            'givenName' => 'Test',
+            'familyName' => 'User',
+        ];
+    }
+
+    public static function getSubmissionData(): array
+    {
+        return [
+            'title' => 'Test Submission',
+            'abstract' => 'Test abstract',
+            'keywords' => ['test', 'submission'],
+        ];
+    }
+}
+```
+
+### Test Database
+
+**Configuration:**
+```
+DB_CONNECTION=sqlite
+DB_DATABASE=:memory:
+```
+
+**Lifecycle:**
+1. Created before each test suite
+2. Populated with fixtures
+3. Destroyed after test completion
+
+---
+
+## Continuous Integration
+
+### GitHub Actions
+
+**File:** `.github/workflows/test.yml`
+
+```yaml
+name: Tests
+
+on: [push, pull_request]
+
+jobs:
+  test:
+    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
+
+    strategy:
+      matrix:
+        php: ['8.2', '8.3']
+
+    steps:
+      - uses: actions/checkout@v3
+
+      - name: Setup PHP
+        uses: shivammathur/setup-php@v2
+        with:
+          php-version: ${{ matrix.php }}
+
+      - name: Install dependencies
+        run: composer install --dev
+
+      - name: Run tests
+        run: composer test
+
+      - name: Run static analysis
+        run: composer phpstan
+
+      - name: Upload coverage
+        uses: codecov/codecov-action@v3
+        with:
+          file: coverage/clover.xml
+```
+
+### Pre-Commit Hooks
+
+**Recommended:**
+```bash
+# .git/hooks/pre-commit
+#!/bin/bash
+
+# Run tests
+composer test
+
+# Run static analysis
+composer phpstan
+
+# Exit with test status
+```
+
+---
+
+## Test Quality Guidelines
+
+### FIRST Principles
+
+- **F**ast: Tests run quickly
+- **I**solated: No shared state
+- **R**epeatable: Same result every time
+- **S**elf-validating: Clear pass/fail
+- **T**imely: Written before/during development
+
+### Test Naming
+
+**Pattern:** `test_{method}_{scenario}_{expected}`
+
+```php
+✅ test_generateUsers_creates_correct_number_of_users()
+✅ test_generateUsers_enforces_minimum_count()
+✅ test_generateUsers_handles_creation_failure()
+❌ testUsers()  // Too vague
+❌ test1()      // Meaningless
+```
+
+### Assertion Guidelines
+
+**One assertion per concept:**
+
+```php
+✅ public function test_generateUsers_creates_users(): void
+{
+    $userIds = $this->generator->generateUsers(5, 1);
+    
+    $this->assertIsArray($userIds);
+    $this->assertCount(5, $userIds);
+    foreach ($userIds as $userId) {
+        $this->assertIsInt($userId);
+        $this->assertGreaterThan(0, $userId);
+    }
+}
+
+❌ // Too many unrelated assertions
+public function test_everything(): void
+{
+    // 50 assertions about different things
+}
+```
+
+### Test Independence
+
+```php
+✅ // Each test sets up its own state
+public function test_foo(): void
+{
+    $this->setupFoo();
+    // Test foo
+}
+
+public function test_bar(): void
+{
+    $this->setupBar();
+    // Test bar
+}
+
+❌ // Tests depend on order
+public function test_foo(): void
+{
+    // Sets up state for test_bar
+}
+
+public function test_bar(): void
+{
+    // Depends on test_foo running first
+}
+```
+
+---
+
+## Debugging Tests
+
+### Verbose Output
+
+```bash
+./vendor/bin/phpunit --verbose
+```
+
+### Specific Test Debugging
+
+```bash
+# Run single test
+./vendor/bin/phpunit --filter test_generateUsers_creates_users
+
+# Run single file
+./vendor/bin/phpunit tests/Unit/UserGeneratorTest.php
+
+# Stop on first failure
+./vendor/bin/phpunit --stop-on-failure
+```
+
+### Coverage for Specific Test
+
+```bash
+./vendor/bin/phpunit --coverage-html=coverage/html tests/Unit/UserGeneratorTest.php
+```
+
+### Common Issues
+
+**Issue: Mockery expectations not met**
+```
+Solution: Verify method calls match expectations
+```
+
+**Issue: Database not reset**
+```
+Solution: Use transactions or truncate between tests
+```
+
+**Issue: Autoloader not found**
+```
+Solution: Run `composer install --dev`
+```
+

+### When to Update Tests
+
+- ✅ Feature changes
+- ✅ Bug fixes
+- ✅ Performance optimizations (if behavior changes)
+- ❌ Refactoring (if behavior unchanged)
+
+### When to Add Tests
+
+- ✅ New features
+- ✅ Bug fixes (regression tests)
+- ✅ Edge cases discovered
+- ✅ Security vulnerabilities
+
+### When to Remove Tests
+
+- ✅ Feature removed
+- ✅ Test no longer relevant
+- ✅ Test duplicated elsewhere
+

+# API Documentation
+
+**Version:** 1.0.0  
+**Last Updated:** 2026-03-26
+
+Complete API reference for the Dummy Data Generator plugin.
+
+---
+
+## Base URL
+
+All API endpoints are relative to:
+```
+{base_url}/{journal_path}/api/v1/users/
+```
+
+**Example:**
+```
+https://ojs.example.com/my-journal/api/v1/users/
+```
+
+---
+
+## Authentication
+
+All endpoints require authentication using OJS API tokens.
+
+### Obtaining API Token
+
+1. Log in as Journal Manager or Site Admin
+2. Go to: **Profile → API Tokens**
+3. Click **Create New Token**
+4. Copy the token (shown only once)
+
+### Using API Token
+
+Include token in Authorization header:
+```bash
+-H "Authorization: Bearer YOUR_API_TOKEN"
+```
+
+### Required Roles
+
+All endpoints require one of these roles:
+- `ROLE_ID_SITE_ADMIN` (Site Administrator)
+- `ROLE_ID_MANAGER` (Journal Manager)
+
+---
+
+## Endpoints
+
+### POST /generate-users
+
+Generate dummy users with author role assignment.
+
+**URL:**
+```
+POST {base_url}/{journal_path}/api/v1/users/generate-users
+```
+
+**Request Body:**
+```json
+{
+  "count": 20
+}
+```
+
+**Parameters:**
+| Parameter | Type | Required | Default | Min | Max |
+|-----------|------|----------|---------|-----|-----|
+| count | integer | No | 10 | 1 | 100 |
+
+**Example Request:**
+```bash
+curl -X POST \
+  -H "Authorization: Bearer YOUR_TOKEN" \
+  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
+  https://ojs.example.com/journal/api/v1/users/generate-users \
+  -d '{"count": 20}'
+```
+
+**Success Response (200 OK):**
+```json
+{
+  "success": true,
+  "created": 20,
+  "userIds": [101, 102, 103, 104, 105],
+  "defaultPassword": "DummyUser123!",
+  "message": "Successfully created 20 users with author role. All passwords set to: DummyUser123!"
+}
+```
+
+**Error Responses:**
+
+**400 Bad Request - Invalid Count:**
+```json
+{
+  "success": false,
+  "error": "Count must be a valid number between 1 and 100"
+}
+```
+
+**400 Bad Request - No Context:**
+```json
+{
+  "success": false,
+  "error": "No context available. Please access this endpoint from a journal context."
+}
+```
+
+**User Data Structure:**
+```json
+{
+  "username": "dummy_user_0_a1b2c3",
+  "email": "dummy.user.0@example.com",
+  "givenName": "John",
+  "familyName": "Smith",
+  "password": "DummyUser123!", // hashed
+  "role": "Author" // assigned to journal
+}
+```
+
+---
+
+### POST /generate-submissions
+
+Generate complete dummy submissions through workflow.
+
+**URL:**
+```
+POST {base_url}/{journal_path}/api/v1/users/generate-submissions
+```
+
+**Request Body:**
+```json
+{
+  "count": 50
+}
+```
+
+**Parameters:**
+| Parameter | Type | Required | Default | Min | Max |
+|-----------|------|----------|---------|-----|-----|
+| count | integer | No | 20 | 1 | 200 |
+
+**Prerequisites:**
+- Users with author role must exist (generate users first)
+
+**Example Request:**
+```bash
+curl -X POST \
+  -H "Authorization: Bearer YOUR_TOKEN" \
+  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
+  https://ojs.example.com/journal/api/v1/users/generate-submissions \
+  -d '{"count": 50}'
+```
+
+**Success Response (200 OK):**
+```json
+{
+  "success": true,
+  "created": 50,
+  "submissionIds": [201, 202, 203, 204, 205],
+  "message": "Successfully created 50 submissions and progressed through workflow to production stage."
+}
+```
+
+**Error Responses:**
+
+**400 Bad Request - No Authors:**
+```json
+{
+  "success": false,
+  "error": "No author users found. Please generate users first."
+}
+```
+
+**Submission Structure:**
+- **Title:** Academic-style title (e.g., "A Study on Machine Learning Applications")
+- **Abstract:** Lorem ipsum text (150-350 words)
+- **Keywords:** 3-5 academic keywords
+- **File:** Placeholder text manuscript
+- **Workflow Stage:** Production (fully progressed)
+- **Author:** Assigned from generated user pool (cyclic)
+
+**Workflow Progression:**
+```
+Submission Created
+    ↓
+EXTERNAL_REVIEW (Decision::EXTERNAL_REVIEW)
+    ↓
+ACCEPT (Decision::ACCEPT)
+    ↓
+SEND_TO_PRODUCTION (Decision::SEND_TO_PRODUCTION)
+```
+
+---
+
+### POST /generate-issue
+
+Create and publish dummy issue with submissions.
+
+**URL:**
+```
+POST {base_url}/{journal_path}/api/v1/users/generate-issue
+```
+
+**Request Body:**
+```json
+{}
+```
+
+**Parameters:** None
+
+**Prerequisites:**
+- Submissions must exist (generate submissions first)
+
+**Example Request:**
+```bash
+curl -X POST \
+  -H "Authorization: Bearer YOUR_TOKEN" \
+  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
+  https://ojs.example.com/journal/api/v1/users/generate-issue
+```
+
+**Success Response (200 OK):**
+```json
+{
+  "success": true,
+  "issueId": 5,
+  "submissionsPublished": 50,
+  "message": "Issue created and published with all submissions."
+}
+```
+
+**Error Responses:**
+
+**400 Bad Request - No Submissions:**
+```json
+{
+  "success": false,
+  "error": "No submissions found. Please generate submissions first."
+}
+```
+
+**500 Internal Server Error:**
+```json
+{
+  "success": false,
+  "error": "Failed to create issue: [error message]"
+}
+```
+
+**Issue Structure:**
+```json
+{
+  "volume": 1,
+  "number": 1,
+  "year": 2024,
+  "title": "Vol. 1 No. 1 (2024)",
+  "description": "Dummy issue generated for testing purposes.",
+  "datePublished": "2024-01-15",
+  "accessStatus": "open_access",
+  "current": true
+}
+```
+
+---
+
+### DELETE /cleanup
+
+Delete all generated dummy data.
+
+**URL:**
+```
+DELETE {base_url}/{journal_path}/api/v1/users/cleanup
+```
+
+**Query Parameters:**
+```
+?confirm=DELETE_ALL_DUMMY_DATA
+```
+
+**Parameters:**
+| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
+|-----------|------|----------|-------------|
+| confirm | string | Yes | Must be exactly `DELETE_ALL_DUMMY_DATA` |
+
+**Example Request:**
+```bash
+curl -X DELETE \
+  -H "Authorization: Bearer YOUR_TOKEN" \
+  "https://ojs.example.com/journal/api/v1/users/cleanup?confirm=DELETE_ALL_DUMMY_DATA"
+```
+
+**Success Response (200 OK):**
+```json
+{
+  "success": true,
+  "deleted": {
+    "users": 20,
+    "submissions": 50,
+    "issues": 1
+  },
+  "message": "Successfully cleaned up all dummy data."
+}
+```
+
+**Error Responses:**
+
+**400 Bad Request - Confirmation Required:**
+```json
+{
+  "success": false,
+  "error": "Confirmation required. Please set 'confirm' parameter to 'DELETE_ALL_DUMMY_DATA'.",
+  "requiredConfirm": "DELETE_ALL_DUMMY_DATA"
+}
+```
+
+**500 Internal Server Error:**
+```json
+{
+  "success": false,
+  "error": "Cleanup failed: [error message]"
+}
+```
+
+**Deletion Order:**
+1. Issues (reference submissions)
+2. Submissions (reference publications)
+3. Users (independent)
+4. Tracking data (context settings)
+
+---
+
+## Error Handling
+
+### Common Error Codes
+
+| Code | Meaning | Cause |
+|------|---------|-------|
+| 200 | OK | Request successful |
+| 400 | Bad Request | Invalid parameters, missing context |
+| 401 | Unauthorized | Missing or invalid API token |
+| 403 | Forbidden | Insufficient permissions |
+| 500 | Internal Server Error | Server-side error |
+
+### Error Response Format
+
+All errors follow this structure:
+```json
+{
+  "success": false,
+  "error": "Human-readable error message"
+}
+```
+
+---
+
+## Rate Limiting
+
+**Current Status:** Not implemented (planned for v1.1.0)
+
+**Recommendation:** Wait 5 minutes between large generation requests to avoid database overload.
+
+---
+
+## Usage Examples
+
+### Complete Workflow
+
+```bash
+# 1. Generate 20 users
+curl -X POST \
+  -H "Authorization: Bearer TOKEN" \
+  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
+  http://ojs.example.com/journal/api/v1/users/generate-users \
+  -d '{"count": 20}'
+
+# 2. Generate 50 submissions
+curl -X POST \
+  -H "Authorization: Bearer TOKEN" \
+  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
+  http://ojs.example.com/journal/api/v1/users/generate-submissions \
+  -d '{"count": 50}'
+
+# 3. Generate and publish issue
+curl -X POST \
+  -H "Authorization: Bearer TOKEN" \
+  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
+  http://ojs.example.com/journal/api/v1/users/generate-issue
+
+# 4. Cleanup (when done)
+curl -X DELETE \
+  -H "Authorization: Bearer TOKEN" \
+  "http://ojs.example.com/journal/api/v1/users/cleanup?confirm=DELETE_ALL_DUMMY_DATA"
+```
+
+### Using JavaScript (Node.js)
+
+```javascript
+const axios = require('axios');
+
+const API_BASE = 'https://ojs.example.com/journal/api/v1/users';
+const TOKEN = 'your-api-token';
+
+const api = axios.create({
+  baseURL: API_BASE,
+  headers: {
+    'Authorization': `Bearer ${TOKEN}`,
+    'Content-Type': 'application/json'
+  }
+});
+
+// Generate users
+async function generateUsers(count = 20) {
+  const response = await api.post('/generate-users', { count });
+  console.log(`Created ${response.data.created} users`);
+  return response.data.userIds;
+}
+
+// Generate submissions
+async function generateSubmissions(count = 50) {
+  const response = await api.post('/generate-submissions', { count });
+  console.log(`Created ${response.data.created} submissions`);
+  return response.data.submissionIds;
+}
+
+// Generate issue
+async function generateIssue() {
+  const response = await api.post('/generate-issue');
+  console.log(`Created issue ${response.data.issueId}`);
+  return response.data.issueId;
+}
+
+// Cleanup
+async function cleanup() {
+  const response = await api.delete('/cleanup', {
+    params: { confirm: 'DELETE_ALL_DUMMY_DATA' }
+  });
+  console.log('Cleanup complete:', response.data.deleted);
+}
+
+// Run complete workflow
+(async () => {
+  await generateUsers(20);
+  await generateSubmissions(50);
+  await generateIssue();
+  // await cleanup(); // Uncomment to delete all data
+})();
+```
+
+### Using Python
+
+```python
+import requests
+
+API_BASE = 'https://ojs.example.com/journal/api/v1/users'
+TOKEN = 'your-api-token'
+
+headers = {
+    'Authorization': f'Bearer {TOKEN}',
+    'Content-Type': 'application/json'
+}
+
+def generate_users(count=20):
+    response = requests.post(
+        f'{API_BASE}/generate-users',
+        headers=headers,
+        json={'count': count}
+    )
+    data = response.json()
+    print(f"Created {data['created']} users")
+    return data['userIds']
+
+def generate_submissions(count=50):
+    response = requests.post(
+        f'{API_BASE}/generate-submissions',
+        headers=headers,
+        json={'count': count}
+    )
+    data = response.json()
+    print(f"Created {data['created']} submissions")
+    return data['submissionIds']
+
+def generate_issue():
+    response = requests.post(
+        f'{API_BASE}/generate-issue',
+        headers=headers
+    )
+    data = response.json()
+    print(f"Created issue {data['issueId']}")
+    return data['issueId']
+
+def cleanup():
+    response = requests.delete(
+        f'{API_BASE}/cleanup',
+        headers=headers,
+        params={'confirm': 'DELETE_ALL_DUMMY_DATA'}
+    )
+    data = response.json()
+    print(f"Cleanup complete: {data['deleted']}")
+
+# Run workflow
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    generate_users(20)
+    generate_submissions(50)
+    generate_issue()
+    # cleanup()  # Uncomment to delete all data
+```
+
+---
+
+## Best Practices
+
+### 1. Generate in Order
+Always generate in this sequence:
+```
+Users → Submissions → Issue → Cleanup
+```
+
+### 2. Test with Small Counts First
+```bash
+# Test with minimal data
+curl -X POST ... -d '{"count": 2}'
+
+# Then generate full dataset
+curl -X POST ... -d '{"count": 50}'
+```
+
+### 3. Backup Before Large Generations
+```bash
+mysqldump -u ojs_user -p ojs_database > backup_before_dummy_data.sql
+```
+
+### 4. Document Generated Data
+Save user IDs and passwords for testing:
+```bash
+curl ... | jq '.userIds' > generated_user_ids.json
+echo "Default Password: DummyUser123!" >> generated_user_ids.json
+```
+
+### 5. Cleanup After Testing
+Always cleanup after completing tests to avoid database bloat.
+
+---
+
+## Troubleshooting
+
+### API Returns 404 Not Found
+
+**Cause:** Plugin not enabled or wrong URL
+
+**Solution:**
+1. Verify plugin is enabled in admin panel
+2. Check URL includes journal path
+3. Clear OJS cache
+
+### API Returns 401 Unauthorized
+
+**Cause:** Invalid or missing API token
+
+**Solution:**
+1. Generate new API token in profile
+2. Include token in Authorization header
+3. Verify token format: `Bearer YOUR_TOKEN`
+
+### API Returns 403 Forbidden
+
+**Cause:** Insufficient permissions
+
+**Solution:**
+1. Ensure user has Manager or Site Admin role
+2. Check role assignment in user profile
+
+### Generation Hangs or Times Out
+
+**Cause:** Large dataset, slow database
+
+**Solution:**
+1. Reduce count parameter
+2. Increase PHP max_execution_time
+3. Check database performance
+

+- **Documentation:** [README.md](README.md)
+- **Installation:** [INSTALLATION.md](INSTALLATION.md)

+# Changelog
+
+All notable changes to the Dummy Data Generator plugin are documented in this file.
+
+The format is based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.0.0/),
+and this project adheres to [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html).
+
+---
+
+## [1.0.0] - 2026-03-26
+
+### Added
+
+#### Core Features
+- **User Generation** - Create 1-100 dummy users with author role assignment
+  - Unique usernames: `dummy_user_{index}_{hash}`
+  - Unique emails: `dummy.user.{index}@example.com`
+  - Random names from predefined lists
+  - Default password: `DummyUser123!` (hashed with PKP PasswordHasher)
+  - Automatic author role assignment to journal context
+
+- **Submission Generation** - Create 1-200 complete submissions
+  - Academic-style titles from topic templates
+  - Lorem ipsum abstracts (150-350 words)
+  - 3-5 random keywords
+  - Placeholder manuscript files
+  - Full workflow progression via Decision APIs:
+    - EXTERNAL_REVIEW decision
+    - ACCEPT decision
+    - SEND_TO_PRODUCTION decision
+  - Cyclic author assignment
+  - Database transaction support
+
+- **Issue Generation** - Create and publish issues
+  - Auto-generated volume/number/year
+  - Title format: "Vol. X No. Y (YEAR)"
+  - Automatic scheduling of all submissions
+  - Issue publishing with fallback mechanism
+  - Sets as current issue for journal
+
+- **Reversible Cleanup** - Delete all generated data
+  - Tracks all created entities in context settings
+  - Explicit confirmation required (`DELETE_ALL_DUMMY_DATA`)
+  - Deletes in correct order: issues → submissions → users
+  - Returns deletion statistics
+
+#### API Endpoints
+- `POST /api/v1/users/generate-users` - Generate dummy users
+- `POST /api/v1/users/generate-submissions` - Generate submissions
+- `POST /api/v1/users/generate-issue` - Generate and publish issue
+- `DELETE /api/v1/users/cleanup` - Cleanup all generated data
+
+#### Documentation
+- README.md - Installation, usage, features, troubleshooting
+- INSTALLATION.md - Detailed installation guide
+- API_DOCUMENTATION.md - Complete API reference
+- CHANGELOG.md - Version history (this file)
+- CONTRIBUTING.md - Contribution guidelines
+- Codebase documentation (7 documents in `.planning/codebase/`)
+
+#### Testing
+- Unit tests for all generator classes
+- Integration tests for API endpoints
+- E2E tests for complete workflows
+- Mock objects for PKP services
+- PHPUnit configuration with coverage reporting
+
+#### Development Tools
+- Composer scripts for testing and analysis
+- PHPStan configuration at max level
+- Git ignore rules for development artifacts
+- Test bootstrap with multiple autoload strategies
+
+### Changed
+
+- None (initial release)
+
+### Deprecated
+
+- None
+
+### Removed
+
+- None (initial release)
+
+### Fixed
+
+- Fixed FakerTest dead code (removed tests for non-existent methods)
+- Ensured all test methods match actual Faker class API
+
+### Security
+
+- Role-based access control (Manager/Site Admin only)
+- Secure password hashing with PKP PasswordHasher
+- Input validation (count limits: 1-100 users, 1-200 submissions)
+- Cleanup confirmation to prevent accidental deletion
+- SQL injection prevention via Repo APIs
+- File upload security via OJS file service
+
+### Known Limitations (v1.0.0)
+
+- **Rate Limiting:** No cooldown between API requests (planned for v1.1.0)
+- **File Types:** Text placeholders only, no PDF generation (planned for v1.2.0)
+- **Translations:** English only (community translations welcomed)
+- **OJS Version:** 3.5.x support only (3.6+ compatibility TBD)
+- **User Invitations:** Bypasses OJS 3.5 invitation system (by design for testing)
+
+---
+
+## [0.1.0] - 2025-02-21
+
+### Added
+
+- Initial development release
+- Basic user generation functionality
+- Submission generation with workflow
+- Issue creation and publication
+- Cleanup functionality
+
+### Notes
+
+This version was a development preview and not suitable for production use.
+Superseded by v1.0.0 with comprehensive testing, documentation, and error handling.
+
+---
+
+## Version Support
+
+| Version | PHP | OJS | Status | End of Support |
+|---------|-----|-----|--------|----------------|
+| 1.0.0 | 8.2+ | 3.5+ | ✅ Active | TBD |
+| 0.1.0 | 8.2+ | 3.5+ | ❌ Deprecated | 2026-03-26 |
+

+
+## Upgrade Guide
+
+### From 0.1.0 to 1.0.0
+
+1. **Backup database:**
+   ```bash
+   mysqldump -u ojs_user -p ojs_database > backup.sql
+   ```
+
+2. **Remove old plugin:**
+   ```bash
+   rm -rf /path/to/ojs/plugins/generic/dummyDataGenerator
+   ```
+
+3. **Install v1.0.0:**
+   ```bash
+   cp -r dummyDataGenerator /path/to/ojs/plugins/generic/
+   ```
+
+4. **Clear cache:**
+   ```bash
+   php tools/clearCache.php
+   ```
+
+5. **Re-enable plugin:**
+   ```bash
+   php tools/plugins.php enable dummyDataGenerator
+   ```
+
+**Breaking Changes:** None  
+**Deprecations:** None  
+**Migration Required:** No
+

+# Contributing Guidelines
+
+Thank you for your interest in contributing to the Dummy Data Generator plugin!
+
+This document provides guidelines and instructions for contributing.
+
+---
+
+## 🎯 How to Contribute
+
+### Reporting Bugs
+
+Before creating bug reports, please check existing issues as you might find the problem already reported.
+
+**When creating a bug report, include:**
+- Clear title and description
+- Steps to reproduce the issue
+- Expected behavior vs actual behavior
+- OJS version and PHP version
+- Any error messages or logs
+- Screenshots if applicable
+
+**Example:**
+```markdown
+**Title:** Submission generation fails with "No context available"
+
+**Description:**
+When generating submissions via API, I get a 400 error.
+
+**Steps to Reproduce:**
+1. Enable plugin
+2. Generate 10 users successfully
+3. Try to generate 5 submissions
+
+**Expected:** Submissions created
+**Actual:** 400 error "No context available"
+
+**Environment:**
+- OJS 3.5.4
+- PHP 8.3
+- Apache 2.4
+
+**Logs:**
+[Error log snippet]
+```
+
+### Suggesting Features
+
+Feature suggestions are welcome! Please create an issue with:
+- Clear description of the feature
+- Use case / problem it solves
+- Examples of how it would work
+- Any alternatives you've considered
+
+### Pull Requests
+
+**Before submitting a PR:**
+1. Fork the repository
+2. Create a branch from `main`
+3. Make your changes
+4. Test thoroughly
+5. Update documentation if needed
+6. Submit PR with clear description
+
+**PR Guidelines:**
+- One feature/fix per PR
+- Follow existing code style (PSR-12)
+- Add/update tests for new functionality
+- Update documentation as needed
+- Use descriptive commit messages
+
+---
+
+## 🛠 Development Setup
+
+### Prerequisites
+
+- PHP 8.2 or later
+- Composer
+- OJS 3.5+ installation
+- Git
+
+### Setup Development Environment
+
+1. **Fork and clone:**
+   ```bash
+   git clone https://github.com/your-username/dummyDataGenerator.git
+   cd dummyDataGenerator
+   ```
+
+2. **Install dependencies:**
+   ```bash
+   composer install --dev
+   ```
+
+3. **Copy to OJS plugins:**
+   ```bash
+   cp -r . /path/to/ojs/plugins/generic/dummyDataGenerator/
+   ```
+
+4. **Enable plugin in OJS**
+
+### Running Tests
+
+```bash
+# All tests
+composer test
+
+# Unit tests only
+composer test
+
+# Integration tests
+composer test:integration
+
+# E2E tests
+composer test:e2e
+
+# With coverage
+composer test:coverage
+```
+
+### Code Quality
+
+```bash
+# Static analysis
+composer phpstan
+
+# Check code style (if PHP CS Fixer added)
+composer cs-check
+
+# Fix code style
+composer cs-fix
+```
+
+---
+
+## 📝 Coding Standards
+
+### PHP Standards
+
+- **PSR-12** coding style
+- **Strict types** enabled (`declare(strict_types=1);`)
+- **Type declarations** for all parameters and returns
+- **PSR-4** autoloading
+
+### File Structure
+
+```
+dummyDataGenerator/
+├── classes/           # Core business logic
+├── api/               # API handlers
+├── tests/             # Test suite
+│   ├── Unit/         # Unit tests
+│   ├── Integration/  # Integration tests
+│   └── E2E/          # E2E tests
+├── locale/            # Translations
+└── docs/              # Documentation
+```
+
+### Naming Conventions
+
+- **Classes:** PascalCase (e.g., `UserGenerator`)
+- **Methods:** camelCase (e.g., `generateUsers()`)
+- **Files:** Match class name (e.g., `UserGenerator.php`)
+- **Tests:** `{ClassName}Test.php` (e.g., `UserGeneratorTest.php`)
+
+### Documentation
+
+All files must include:
+- File header with copyright, license, author
+- Class/method DocBlocks
+- `@since` tag for version
+- `@param` and `@return` for methods
+
+**Example:**
+```php
+<?php
+/**
+ * @file UserGenerator.php
+ *
+ * Copyright (c) 2025 Munir Abbasi
+ * Distributed under the GNU GPL v3.
+ *
+ * @brief Generate dummy users with author role assignment
+ *
+ * @author Munir Abbasi <munir@syntaxhouse.com>
+ * @since 3.5.0
+ */
+
+declare(strict_types=1);
+
+namespace APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\classes;
+
+/**
+ * Generate dummy users
+ */
+class UserGenerator
+{
+    /**
+     * Generate multiple dummy users
+     *
+     * @param int $count Number of users
+     * @param int $contextId Context ID
+     * @return array User IDs
+     */
+    public function generateUsers(int $count, int $contextId): array
+    {
+        // Implementation
+    }
+}
+```
+
+---
+
+## 🧪 Testing Guidelines
+
+### Writing Tests
+
+**Unit Tests:**
+- Test one thing per method
+- Use descriptive names: `test_generateUsers_creates_correct_number_of_users()`
+- Mock all external dependencies
+- Keep tests fast (<1ms each)
+
+**Integration Tests:**
+- Test API endpoints
+- Use in-memory database
+- Test multiple components together
+
+**E2E Tests:**
+- Test complete workflows
+- Simulate real API usage
+- Verify end state
+
+### Test Coverage
+
+Target coverage:
+- UserGenerator: 90%
+- SubmissionGenerator: 90%
+- IssueGenerator: 85%
+- DataTracker: 95%
+- Faker: 80%
+- APIHandler: 85%
+- Overall: ≥85%
+
+---
+
+## 🌍 Translations
+
+We welcome translations for all languages!
+
+### Adding a Translation
+
+1. Create directory: `locale/{lang_code}/`
+2. Copy `locale/en/default.po` to new directory
+3. Translate all `msgstr` values
+4. Test in OJS with that locale
+5. Submit PR
+
+**Example:**
+```bash
+mkdir -p locale/fr/
+cp locale/en/default.po locale/fr/
+# Edit locale/fr/default.po with French translations
+```
+
+### Translation Guidelines
+
+- Keep `msgid` in English
+- Translate `msgstr` to target language
+- Preserve formatting and placeholders
+- Test all translated strings in context
+
+**Example:**
+```po
+msgid "plugins.generic.dummyDataGenerator.success.usersCreated"
+msgstr "Successfully created {$count} users with author role..."
+
+# French translation:
+msgid "plugins.generic.dummyDataGenerator.success.usersCreated"
+msgstr "{$count} utilisateurs créés avec succès avec le rôle d'auteur..."
+```
+

+- Inline code comments (if complex logic)

+### Documentation Style
+
+- Clear and concise

+## 🔀 Git Workflow
+
+### Branch Naming
+
+```
+feature/add-rate-limiting
+fix/submission-workflow-error
+docs/update-installation-guide
+refactor/user-generator-class
+```
+
+### Commit Messages
+
+Follow [Conventional Commits](https://www.conventionalcommits.org/):
+
+```
+type(scope): subject
+
+body (optional)
+
+footer (optional)
+```
+
+**Types:**
+- `feat`: New feature
+- `fix`: Bug fix
+- `docs`: Documentation
+- `style`: Code style (formatting)
+- `refactor`: Code restructuring
+- `test`: Tests
+- `chore`: Maintenance
+
+**Examples:**
+```
+feat(api): add rate limiting to generate-users endpoint
+
+Implement 5-minute cooldown between generation requests.
+Store last request timestamp in context settings.
+
+Closes #44
+
+---
+
+fix(submissions): handle missing genre gracefully
+
+Add fallback to default genre if "Article Text" not found.
+Log warning when fallback used.
+
+Fixes #29
+
+---
+
+docs(readme): add troubleshooting section
+
+Include common installation issues and solutions.
+Add error code reference table.
+```
+
+### Pull Request Process
+
+1. **Create branch from `main`:**
+   ```bash
+   git checkout main
+   git pull origin main
+   git checkout -b feature/your-feature
+   ```
+
+2. **Make changes and commit:**
+   ```bash
+   git add .
+   git commit -m "feat: add your feature"
+   ```
+
+3. **Push to your fork:**
+   ```bash
+   git push origin feature/your-feature
+   ```
+
+4. **Create PR on GitHub:**
+   - Go to repository
+   - Click "Pull Requests"
+   - Click "New Pull Request"
+   - Select your branch
+   - Fill in PR template
+
+5. **Code review:**
+   - Maintainer reviews code
+   - Address feedback
+   - Push fixes to same branch
+
+6. **Merge:**
+   - Maintainer merges PR
+   - Branch deleted
+

+
+## Decision
+
+What is the change that we're proposing and/or doing?
+
+## Consequences
+
+What becomes easier or more difficult to do because of this change?
+```

+<?php
+/**
+ * @file DummyDataGeneratorPlugin.php
+ *
+ * Copyright (c) 2025 Munir Abbasi
+ * Distributed under the GNU GPL v3.
+ *
+ * @class DummyDataGeneratorPlugin
+ * @brief Generate dummy users, submissions, and issues for OJS development/testing
+ *
+ * @author Munir Abbasi <munir@syntaxhouse.com>
+ * @link https://github.com/munir-abbasi/dummyDataGenerator
+ * @since 3.5.0
+ */
+
+declare(strict_types=1);
+
+namespace APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator;
+
+use APP\core\Application;
+use APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\api\DummyDataAPIHandler;
+use APP\template\TemplateManager;
+use PKP\plugins\GenericPlugin;
+use PKP\plugins\Hook;
+use PKP\security\Role;
+
+class DummyDataGeneratorPlugin extends GenericPlugin
+{
+    /**
+     * @copydoc Plugin::register()
+     */
+    public function register($category, $path, $mainContextId = null): bool
+    {
+        $success = parent::register($category, $path, $mainContextId);
+
+        if ($success && $this->getEnabled($mainContextId)) {
+            // Only register API routes if we have a valid context
+            if ($mainContextId !== null) {
+                $this->registerAPIHandler();
+            }
+        }
+
+        return $success;
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Register custom API handler for dummy data operations
+     * Extends the users API endpoint with custom routes
+     */
+    private function registerAPIHandler(): void
+    {
+        // Register custom API handler that extends PKPUserController
+        Hook::add('APIHandler::users', function (string $hookName, $apiHandler): bool {
+            $controller = new DummyDataAPIHandler($this);
+            
+            // Add our custom routes to the existing handler
+            $apiHandler->addRoute(
+                'POST',
+                'generate-users',
+                [$controller, 'generateUsers'],
+                'dummyData.generateUsers',
+                [Role::ROLE_ID_SITE_ADMIN, Role::ROLE_ID_MANAGER]
+            );
+
+            $apiHandler->addRoute(
+                'POST',
+                'generate-submissions',
+                [$controller, 'generateSubmissions'],
+                'dummyData.generateSubmissions',
+                [Role::ROLE_ID_SITE_ADMIN, Role::ROLE_ID_MANAGER]
+            );
+
+            $apiHandler->addRoute(
+                'POST',
+                'generate-issue',
+                [$controller, 'generateIssue'],
+                'dummyData.generateIssue',
+                [Role::ROLE_ID_SITE_ADMIN, Role::ROLE_ID_MANAGER]
+            );
+
+            $apiHandler->addRoute(
+                'DELETE',
+                'cleanup',
+                [$controller, 'cleanup'],
+                'dummyData.cleanup',
+                [Role::ROLE_ID_SITE_ADMIN, Role::ROLE_ID_MANAGER]
+            );
+
+            return Hook::CONTINUE;
+        });
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * @copydoc Plugin::getDisplayName()
+     */
+    public function getDisplayName(): string
+    {
+        return __('plugins.generic.dummyDataGenerator.displayName');
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * @copydoc Plugin::getDescription()
+     */
+    public function getDescription(): string
+    {
+        return __('plugins.generic.dummyDataGenerator.description');
+    }
+}
+
+// For backwards compatibility -- expect this to be removed approx. OJS/OMP/OPS 3.6
+if (!PKP_STRICT_MODE) {
+    class_alias('\APP\plugins\generic\dummyDataGenerator\DummyDataGeneratorPlugin', '\DummyDataGeneratorPlugin');
+}

+# Installation Guide
+
+**Version:** 1.0.0  
+**Last Updated:** 2026-03-26
+
+This guide covers installation of the Dummy Data Generator plugin for OJS 3.5+.
+
+---
+
+## Prerequisites
+
+Before installing, ensure you have:
+
+- [x] OJS 3.5.0 or later installed
+- [x] PHP 8.2 or later
+- [x] Journal Manager or Site Administrator access
+- [x] Write access to OJS installation directory
+
+---
+
+## Installation Steps
+
+### Step 1: Download Plugin
+
+**Option A: Clone from GitHub**
+```bash
+cd /tmp
+git clone https://github.com/munir-abbasi/dummyDataGenerator.git
+```
+
+**Option B: Download ZIP**
+```bash
+cd /tmp
+wget https://github.com/munir-abbasi/dummyDataGenerator/archive/refs/heads/main.zip
+unzip main.zip
+mv dummyDataGenerator-main dummyDataGenerator
+```
+
+**Option C: Manual Copy**
+Copy the `dummyDataGenerator` directory from your local development environment.
+
+---
+
+### Step 2: Copy to OJS Plugins Directory
+
+Copy the plugin to your OJS installation:
+
+```bash
+cp -r dummyDataGenerator /path/to/ojs/plugins/generic/
+```
+
+**Verify directory structure:**
+```
+/path/to/ojs/plugins/generic/dummyDataGenerator/
+├── DummyDataGeneratorPlugin.php
+├── api/
+├── classes/
+├── locale/
+├── tests/
+├── composer.json
+└── version.xml
+```
+
+---
+
+### Step 3: Set Permissions
+
+Ensure the plugin files are readable by the web server:
+
+```bash
+cd /path/to/ojs/plugins/generic/
+chown -R www-data:www-data dummyDataGenerator/
+chmod -R 755 dummyDataGenerator/
+```
+
+**Note:** Replace `www-data` with your web server user (e.g., `apache`, `nginx`, `http`).
+
+---
+
+### Step 4: Clear OJS Cache
+
+Clear the template and data cache:
+
+```bash
+cd /path/to/ojs
+php tools/clearCache.php
+```
+
+**Alternative:** Delete cache manually:
+```bash
+rm -rf /path/to/ojs/cache/*
+```
+
+---
+
+### Step 5: Enable Plugin
+
+**Method A: Via Web Interface (Recommended)**
+
+1. Log in as Journal Manager or Site Admin
+2. Navigate to: **Website Administration → Settings → Website → Plugins**
+3. Find **"Dummy Data Generator"** in the plugin list
+4. Check the enable checkbox
+5. Click **Save**
+
+**Method B: Via CLI**
+
+```bash
+cd /path/to/ojs
+php tools/plugins.php enable dummyDataGenerator
+```
+
+**Verify plugin is enabled:**
+```bash
+php tools/plugins.php list | grep dummyDataGenerator
+```
+
+---
+
+### Step 6: Verify Installation
+
+**Check plugin appears in admin panel:**
+1. Go to Website Administration → Settings → Website → Plugins
+2. Verify "Dummy Data Generator" is listed and enabled
+
+**Test API endpoint:**
+```bash
+curl -X POST \
+  -H "Authorization: Bearer YOUR_API_TOKEN" \
+  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
+  http://your-ojs-url/api/v1/users/generate-users \
+  -d '{"count": 1}'
+```
+
+**Expected response:**
+```json
+{
+  "success": true,
+  "created": 1,
+  "userIds": [1],
+  "defaultPassword": "DummyUser123!"
+}
+```
+
+---
+
+## Troubleshooting
+
+### Plugin Not Showing in List
+
+**Symptoms:** Plugin doesn't appear in the plugin gallery
+
+**Solutions:**
+1. **Verify directory location:**
+   ```bash
+   ls -la /path/to/ojs/plugins/generic/dummyDataGenerator/
+   ```
+
+2. **Check version.xml exists and is valid:**
+   ```bash
+   cat /path/to/ojs/plugins/generic/dummyDataGenerator/version.xml
+   ```
+
+3. **Clear template cache:**
+   ```bash
+   cd /path/to/ojs
+   php tools/clearCache.php
+   ```
+
+4. **Check file permissions:**
+   ```bash
+   find /path/to/ojs/plugins/generic/dummyDataGenerator -type f -exec ls -la {} \;
+   ```
+
+---
+
+### Error: "No context available"
+
+**Symptoms:** API returns 400 error with "No context available" message
+
+**Cause:** API endpoint accessed without journal context
+
+**Solution:** Include journal path in URL:
+```bash
+# ❌ Wrong (no context)
+http://your-ojs-url/api/v1/users/generate-users
+
+# ✅ Correct (with journal context)
+http://your-ojs-url/my-journal/api/v1/users/generate-users
+```
+
+---
+
+### Error: "No author users found"
+
+**Symptoms:** Submission generation fails
+
+**Cause:** No users with author role exist
+
+**Solution:** Generate users first:
+```bash
+# Step 1: Generate users
+curl -X POST http://your-ojs-url/journal/api/v1/users/generate-users \
+  -H "Authorization: Bearer TOKEN" \
+  -d '{"count": 10}'
+
+# Step 2: Generate submissions
+curl -X POST http://your-ojs-url/journal/api/v1/users/generate-submissions \
+  -H "Authorization: Bearer TOKEN" \
+  -d '{"count": 5}'
+```
+
+---
+
+### Error: "Decision validation failed"
+
+**Symptoms:** Submissions created but workflow doesn't progress
+
+**Cause:** Missing editor permissions or incorrect workflow stage
+
+**Solution:**
+1. Ensure API token belongs to Manager or Site Admin
+2. Verify journal has editorial workflow configured
+3. Check OJS logs for detailed error:
+   ```bash
+   tail -f /path/to/ojs/logs/error.log
+   ```
+
+---
+
+### Plugin Causes White Screen / 500 Error
+
+**Symptoms:** OJS crashes after enabling plugin
+
+**Cause:** PHP syntax error or compatibility issue
+
+**Solution:**
+1. **Disable plugin via database:**
+   ```sql
+   UPDATE versions SET current = 0 WHERE product = 'dummyDataGenerator';
+   UPDATE plugin_settings SET setting_value = 0 WHERE setting_name = 'enabled';
+   ```
+
+2. **Check PHP version:**
+   ```bash
+   php -v
+   # Should be 8.2+
+   ```
+
+3. **Check error logs:**
+   ```bash
+   tail -f /path/to/ojs/logs/error.log
+   ```
+
+4. **Verify OJS version:**
+   ```bash
+   cd /path/to/ojs
+   php tools/clearCache.php
+   ```
+
+---
+
+### Composer Dependencies Missing
+
+**Symptoms:** Tests fail, autoloader not found
+
+**Solution:**
+```bash
+cd /path/to/ojs/plugins/generic/dummyDataGenerator
+composer install --dev
+```
+
+**Note:** Composer dependencies are only required for development/testing, not runtime.
+
+---
+
+## Uninstallation
+
+### Step 1: Cleanup Generated Data
+
+Delete all dummy data before uninstalling:
+
+```bash
+curl -X DELETE \
+  -H "Authorization: Bearer YOUR_API_TOKEN" \
+  http://your-ojs-url/journal/api/v1/users/cleanup?confirm=DELETE_ALL_DUMMY_DATA
+```
+
+### Step 2: Disable Plugin
+
+**Via Web Interface:**
+1. Website Administration → Settings → Website → Plugins
+2. Uncheck "Dummy Data Generator"
+3. Click **Save**
+
+**Via CLI:**
+```bash
+cd /path/to/ojs
+php tools/plugins.php disable dummyDataGenerator
+```
+
+### Step 3: Remove Plugin Files
+
+```bash
+rm -rf /path/to/ojs/plugins/generic/dummyDataGenerator
+```
+
+### Step 4: Clear Cache
+
+```bash
+cd /path/to/ojs
+php tools/clearCache.php
+```
+
+---
+
+## Multi-Journal Installation
+
+The plugin works with OJS multi-journal installations. Each journal context has isolated data:
+
+- Users are assigned to specific journals
+- Submissions belong to journal sections
+- Issues are journal-specific
+- Tracking data is per-journal
+
+**No special configuration needed** - the plugin automatically detects the journal context from the API URL.
+
+---
+
+## Upgrade from Previous Versions
+
+### From v0.1.0 to v1.0.0
+
+1. **Backup database:**
+   ```bash
+   mysqldump -u ojs_user -p ojs_database > backup.sql
+   ```
+
+2. **Remove old plugin:**
+   ```bash
+   rm -rf /path/to/ojs/plugins/generic/dummyDataGenerator
+   ```
+
+3. **Install new version:**
+   ```bash
+   cp -r dummyDataGenerator /path/to/ojs/plugins/generic/
+   ```
+
+4. **Clear cache and re-enable:**
+   ```bash
+   php tools/clearCache.php
+   php tools/plugins.php enable dummyDataGenerator
+   ```
+
+---
+
+## Post-Installation Checklist
+
+- [ ] Plugin enabled and visible in admin panel
+- [ ] API endpoints accessible with valid token
+- [ ] Test user generation (create 1-2 users)
+- [ ] Verify users have author role assigned
+- [ ] Test submission generation (create 1 submission)
+- [ ] Verify workflow progression
+- [ ] Test cleanup (delete test data)
+- [ ] Document API token for team members
+- [ ] Review security considerations
+
+---
+
+## Support
+
+If you encounter issues not covered in this guide:
+
+1. **Check logs:** `/path/to/ojs/logs/error.log`
+2. **Review requirements:** Ensure OJS 3.5+ and PHP 8.2+
+3. **GitHub Issues:** [Create an issue](https://github.com/munir-abbasi/dummyDataGenerator/issues)
+4. **PKP Forum:** [Post in Community Forum](https://forum.pkp.sfu.ca)
+
+---
+
+**Next Steps:** After successful installation, proceed to [API_DOCUMENTATION.md](API_DOCUMENTATION.md) for usage instructions.
